# Hermes Desktop parity matrix

Generated by `scripts/parity-matrix.py` from [`docs/PARITY.md`](../PARITY.md) (source of truth for the feature list and status) plus `docs/parity/annotations/*.json`. Do not edit by hand — edit PARITY.md or an annotation file and regenerate.

**854 rows** · done 21 · partial 38 · missing 794 · unsupported 1 · behavior annotated 854

Columns: **Behavior** is `trigger → result`, written from the Electron source. **States** lists the visual/interaction states the element has in the original. **Screens** are files under `docs/parity/screens/`. **Unsure** flags anything the analyst could not confirm from source or observation.

| UPD-001 | Auto-updater (macOS electron-updater) | macOS auto-updater (electron-updater): the app checks the configured feed, downloads in the background and offers 'Update now' / 'Maybe later', with staged copy 'Downloading…', 'Almost there…', 'Finishing up…'. On Linux there is no electron-updater path — this is macOS-only packaging behaviour. | local (Electron) (platform) · `electron/app-updater.ts, electron/updater/mac.ts` | checking, available, downloading, ready to install, error, up to date | — | Not testable in the Rust app: native Linux builds update by package or bundled runtime; verify the app reports 'This version of Hermes can't update itself from inside the app.' when unsupported. | missing | Linux behaviour is derived from the Linux packaging (AppImage/.deb/.rpm) referenced in the guiSkew copy; I did not read electron/updater/mac.ts internals. |

Windows-only. | local (Electron) (platform) · `electron/appinstaller-checker.ts` | checking, available, applying, check failed | — | Not testable on Linux; the equivalent check for the Rust app is to verify it does not claim an in-app self-update on Linux. | missing | The feed URL and AppInstaller behaviour were not read in full; the Linux answer for this row is 'not supported'. |
